# OSV advisories (non-GHSA) — Android 2023 (746 shown; all 746 in `data/osv/Android/2023.jsonl`)

### ASB-A-204584366 — 
- Aliases: A-204584366, CVE-2023-20920 | Published: 2023-01-01 | Modified: 2026-11-11 | Severity: None
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/base: introduced:10:0 fixed:10:2023-01-01 versions: 10
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/base: introduced:11:0 fixed:11:2023-01-01 versions: 11
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/base: introduced:12:0 fixed:12:2023-01-01 versions: 12
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/base: introduced:12L:0 fixed:12L:2023-01-01 versions: 12L
- Affected Android/platform/frameworks/base: introduced:13:0 fixed:13:2023-01-01 versions: 13

In queue of UsbRequest.java, there is a possible way to corrupt memory due to a use after free. This could lead to local escalation of privilege with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

References:
- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2023-01-01 [ADVISORY]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/base/+/d8e918fc9d479388661e74638d950592af16c001 [FIX]

### ASB-A-228602963 — 
- Aliases: A-228602963, CVE-2022-20461 | Published: 2023-01-01 | Modified: 2026-11-11 | Severity: None
- Affected Android/platform/system/bt: introduced:10:0 fixed:10:2023-01-01 versions: 10

- Affected Android/platform/system/bt: introduced:12:0 fixed:12:2023-01-01 versions: 12
- Affected Android/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth: introduced:13:0 fixed:13:2023-01-01 versions: 13

In pinReplyNative of com_android_bluetooth_btservice_AdapterService.cpp, there is a possible out of bounds read due to type confusion. This could lead to local escalation of privilege of BLE with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

References:
- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2023-01-01 [ADVISORY]
- https://android.googlesource.com/platform/packages/modules/Bluetooth/+/2c09d202c83af6a511ad29ead37f4055760f095f [FIX]

### ASB-A-259967780 — 
- Aliases: A-259967780, CVE-2022-20235 | Published: 2023-01-01 | Modified: 2026-11-11 | Severity: None
- Affected Android/:unknown:: introduced:SoCVersion:0 fixed:SoCVersion:2023-01-05 versions: SoCVersion

The PowerVR GPU kernel driver maintains an "Information Page" used by its cache subsystem. This page can only be written by the GPU driver itself, but prior to DDK 1.18 however, a user-space program could write arbitrary data to the page, leading to memory corruption issues.

- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2023-01-01 [ADVISORY]

- Affected Android/:unknown:: introduced:SoCVersion:0 fixed:SoCVersion:2023-04-05 versions: SoCVersion

In PVRSRVBridgeChangeSparseMem of the PowerVR kernel driver, a missing size check means there is a possible integer overflow that could allow out-of-bounds heap access. This could lead to local escalation of privilege with no additional execution privileges needed. User interaction is not needed for exploitation.

References:
- https://source.android.com/security/bulletin/2023-04-01 [ADVISORY]
